## 2026-06-12T01:18:14Z - Claude Opus 4.8 - Ralph iteration 3: Phase 1 design tokens
- Implemented the full `design_tokens.json` set in `src/index.css` as Tailwind v4 `@theme static`.
- Colors (17): background, surface, surface-subtle, border, text, muted, muted-subtle, primary(+subtle), success(+subtle), warning(+subtle), danger(+subtle), purple(+subtle). Utilities: `bg-*`/`text-*`/`border-*` etc.
- Typography: `--font-sans` (Inter); sizes xs=12 sm=13 md=14 lg=16 xl=20 2xl=24 (px, matching the canonical tokens exactly — note: px not rem, so they don't scale with user font settings; revisit if a11y wants rem). Weights regular..extrabold → `font-regular`/`medium`/`semibold`/`bold`/`extrabold`.
- Radii: `rounded-button` (8px), `rounded-card` (12px).

- GOTCHA (Tailwind v4): plain `@theme` TREE-SHAKES unused theme vars out of the build — `color-purple`/`surface-subtle` were missing until referenced. FIX: `@theme static` always emits the declared token set (verified all present in dist CSS). Use this for canonical token layers.
- Gate green: typecheck, `pnpm test` (3/3), format:check, build (CSS 9.02 kB).

## 2026-06-12T02:10:26Z - Claude Opus 4.8 - Ralph iteration 12: Phase 3 storage services
- Phase 3 services done (quota / persistent-request / health). **Phase 3 complete except "Add migrations"** (deferred — baseline v1, no migration yet).
- `src/services/storageService.ts` (new `src/services/` dir): `estimateStorage` (navigator.storage.estimate → {usedBytes,quotaBytes}), `isStoragePersisted`, `requestPersistentStorage`, `assessStorageHealth(usage,persisted)` → {level ok|warning|critical, usedRatio, persisted, recommendations[]} (thresholds: ≥0.9 critical, ≥0.75 warning; not-persisted → at least warning + recommendation), `refreshStorageInfo(dispatch, manager?)` dispatches storageEstimateSet + storagePersistedSet and returns health.
- DESIGN: StorageManager is injectable (param defaults to navigator.storage) so it's unit-testable without a browser and degrades to zeros/false when the API is absent. This is the pattern for all browser-API services.

## 2026-06-12T14:15:47Z - Claude Opus 4.8 - IndexedDB model-blob cache (wllama hardening) + load coalescing
- WHY: Firefox can't use wllama's OPFS cache, so the blob fallback re-downloaded the GGUF every session. User asked: "Add an IndexedDB blob cache."
- Dexie schema **v2 migration** (DB_VERSION 1→2): new `model_blobs` store (`modelId, cachedAt`), `ModelBlobRow {modelId, blob: Blob, cachedAt}` in `src/db/types.ts`. Used `this.version(1).stores({...all 17...}); this.version(2).stores({ model_blobs: '...' })` — Dexie carries v1 tables forward, v2 declares only the delta. Now 18 stores (`db.test.ts` updated 17→18).
- NEW `src/wllama/modelCache.ts`: `ModelBlobStore` iface + `createDexieModelBlobStore(db)` (get/put/delete on `db.model_blobs`), `fetchGguf()` (moved here, injectable `fetchImpl`), `getOrFetchModelBlob(store, model, {fetchImpl?, onProgress?})` = cache hit returns blob, miss downloads + `store.put`. Unit-tested in `modelCache.test.ts` (round-trip + download-once-then-cache-hit). **Test needs `// @vitest-environment node`** — jsdom's Blob doesn't round-trip through fake-indexeddb's structuredClone, and undici's Response rejects jsdom Blobs ("object.stream is not a function").
- `engine.ts`: `WllamaEngineOptions {blobStore?}`; OPFS-error fallback now calls `getOrFetchModelBlob` (was bare `fetchGguf`); `deleteCache(modelId?)` deletes the cached blob; `getWllamaEngine()` wires `createDexieModelBlobStore(db)`. `modelManager.remove` → `engine.deleteCache(model.id)`.
- **BUG found + fixed: OPFS double-handle collision on Chromium.** After pointing WllamaProbe at the singleton `getWllamaEngine()`, React **StrictMode**'s double-effect called `loadModelFromHF` twice on the SAME wllama instance → Chromium threw `createSyncAccessHandle ... another open Access Handle`. FIX (production-valuable): `engine.loadFromHF` now **coalesces** concurrent same-model loads via an `inFlight {id, promise}` guard (runLoad does the real work); also `WllamaProbe`/`ModelCacheProbe` use a `useRef` one-shot guard. The old per-call engine had masked this real race.
- **Verification lesson: don't assert on wllama's flaky OPFS.** First attempt asserted `model_blobs > 0` after a wllama load on Firefox — FLAKY: Firefox OPFS sometimes WORKS (then the blob fallback never runs, count=0). Replaced with a **deterministic** probe: NEW `src/screens/ModelCacheProbe.tsx` + route `/model-cache-probe` drives `getOrFetchModelBlob` directly (no wllama/OPFS), and `wllama.extended.spec.ts` counts `huggingface.co/.../resolve/` requests → downloads once, 0 new requests after reload. Runs in BOTH browsers.
- Extended e2e GREEN both browsers (4 passed, 3.5m): wllama download+inference (chromium 1.8m, firefox 2.7m) + blob-cache-persists-across-reload (chromium 47s, firefox 50s). Unit gate green: typecheck, lint, format, **154 vitest** (was 152, +2 modelCache), build.
- STILL deferred (not requested now): "vendor the wasm" = copy `node_modules/@wllama/wllama/esm/wasm/wllama.wasm` into the app + serve via Vite `?url` instead of the jsdelivr CDN (removes the runtime CDN dependency). WASM_URL is still the CDN.

Success with snapshot → audit 'runtime.snapshot_restored' (info/success). ## 2026-06-12T22:00:20Z - Claude Opus 4.8 - Hardening Pass 11 (Phase 7.2): skill filesystem path safety + reserved system-state keys
- WHY (chosen over 2.3-UI and 5.1): self-contained pure-logic security task, no routes/store-coupling/WASM. Old isPathAllowed only checked `path.includes('..')` and STRIPPED leading '/' (so absolute paths were normalized-and-allowed); skill_state had NO key protection, so a skill could fs.setState('__permissions__', …) and OVERWRITE its own permissions row (escalation). [NOTE: 5.1 storage handlers deferred — runtime's storage_put(store:'messages') lacks conversationId + double-writes vs llmRunner; needs a claw-schema/WASM contract decision first, NOT a clean bounded task. 2.3 secrets UI deferred — no /security route in canonical nav; inline-on-Models couples to the vault singleton's app-store-bound observer, needs care.]
- skillFs.ts: NEW `isPathSafe(path)` — rejects empty, null byte (\0 + %00), backslash (\\), absolute (startsWith '/'), '.'/'..' segments, AND malformed/percent-encoded traversal: decodeURIComponent in try/catch (malformed %ZZ → reject), then checks BOTH raw + decoded forms for the same escapes (so %2e%2e / %2e%2e%2f can't slip past). isPathAllowed now = isPathSafe(path) && namespace-prefix match (no more leading-slash stripping → absolute rejected, not normalized-in).
- NEW `RESERVED_STATE_KEYS` = [__permissions__,__manifest__,__system__,__audit__,__secrets__]; `isReservedStateKey(key)` = key.startsWith('__') (whole `__…__` namespace reserved → future system keys safe by default). SkillFs.getState AND setState now throw `…reserved…` for any '__'-prefixed key. So skill can't read __secrets__/__system__ nor overwrite __permissions__. Manager still reads/writes __permissions__ via RAW db.skill_state (createSkillManager.fsFor / install), bypassing the SkillFs guard — only the skill-facing API is gated. "Move permissions out of mutable skill state" satisfied via reserved-row protection (perms still physically in skill_state['__permissions__'] but unreachable by the skill).
- skillFs.test REWRITE: kept original isPathAllowed cases; added isPathSafe matrix (8 escape techniques incl. encoded/null/backslash/malformed), isReservedStateKey, and SkillFs runtime-enforcement (traversal denied on read+write, in-namespace round-trip + state, reserved read/write throws + __permissions__ row untouched after rejected write). Existing skillManager.test fs.setState('count'…) unaffected (non-reserved).
- Gate GREEN: typecheck, lint, 219 vitest (+6), 26 e2e both browsers. No Rust/WASM change.
- DEFERRED in 7.2: "package files read-only" (needs a package-file flag on SkillFileRow + writeText guard) — left unchecked.

Phase 7.3 install/reinstall (stale files removed, enable/disable fails if skill missing, audit only true success). Phase 7.4 runtime permission enforcement (skill can't call undeclared tool/path — tie tool_call to permissions.tools). Phase 6.1 backup validation (manifest schema, allowlist, row validators, size/count limits, reject raw-secret-looking) — check for existing backup service first. ## 2026-06-13T06:20:32Z - Claude Opus 4.8 - Pass 27: Phase 4 snapshot compatibility check (version gate)
- **Bounded task: snapshot schema-version gate.** Before this pass, loadLatestSnapshot returned the raw snapshot regardless of version, and a bad snapshot stayed in IndexedDB to fail forever — a snapshot from an incompatible runtime could silently restore wrong state (anti-deception violation).
- **What I built:** `SNAPSHOT_SCHEMA_VERSION = 1` in src/runtime/referenceRuntime.ts (home of RuntimeSnapshot; NOT runtimeHost.ts — that would make a circular import since runtimeHost imports executeEffect from effectExecutor and effectExecutor needed the const). Both writers stamp it: RuntimeHost.saveSnapshot (id 'latest') + effectExecutor 'runtime_snapshot_save' handler. `RuntimeSnapshotRow.version?: number` added (optional so pre-versioning rows read back undefined). `loadLatestSnapshot` now returns a discriminated `SnapshotLoad = {status:'none'} | {status:'ok',snapshot} | {status:'incompatible',foundVersion}`; on version mismatch (incl. undefined/unversioned) it DELETEs the row and returns 'incompatible'. main.tsx bootRuntime handles the union: 'incompatible' -> appendAudit('runtime.snapshot_incompatible', ...) + start fresh; 'ok' -> restore.
- **Ripple:** changing loadLatestSnapshot's return type touched 3 call sites (main.tsx + 2 in runtimeHost.test.ts — updated to unwrap `.status==='ok' && .snapshot`). Added 2 guardrail tests: "discards an incompatible snapshot" (version+1 -> incompatible + row deleted) and "treats a pre-versioning snapshot as incompatible" (no version field -> incompatible + deleted). Pre-versioning rows being discarded on first upgrade is the CORRECT honest behavior (can't prove compatible -> don't trust).
- **Deliberately NOT done (noted in TODO):** per-effect/idle/error save triggers (blocked on effect-port contract, Phase 1.2); 'snapshot_saved' audit (would spam the log every turn); VISIBLE UI restore warning (incompatible/restore_failed are audited + console-logged but not surfaced as a toast/banner yet — this is the clear NEXT Phase-4 task, self-contained, needs a Redux signal + a banner/toast in AppLayout or the chat).
- Tests +2 (279->281). Gate GREEN: typecheck, lint --max-warnings 0, 281 vitest, 28 e2e both browsers. No Rust changes.
- **NEXT candidates:** Phase 4 "visible restore warning" (self-contained UI follow-up to this pass). Then genuinely-new: Phase 8 (wllama model-manager download honesty), Phase 9 (onboarding/settings/models persistence). Still contract-blocked: 1.2 effect-ports-fatal + 5.1 storage + 7.4 tool (need claw-schema/WASM conversationId/skill_id on effects), which also gates the per-effect snapshot triggers + effect-lifecycle audit (3.3).

FIX: serialize writes through a `useRef<Promise>` chain (writeChain.current = writeChain.current.catch(()=>undefined).then(()=>setOnboardingProgress(...))) so fire-order == resolve-order, latest state wins. ## 2026-06-14T20:38:21Z - Claude Opus 4.8 - Memory RETRIEVAL pipeline (TS-only, in llmRunner)
- Wired real memory retrieval into the LLM turn, entirely in TS (no Rust/WASM change). New pure module src/memories/retrieveMemories.ts: selectMemoriesForContext(memories, queryText, {limit=5}) ranks by keyword overlap (tokens len>=3) with the user's latest message; pinned memories are always candidates (ranked first); SENSITIVE memories are NEVER surfaced (security default — they must not leak into a provider prompt). formatMemoryContext() renders the chosen rows as a "Relevant saved memories" block.

## 2026-06-14T21:58:37Z - Claude Opus 4.8 - Theme setting wired end-to-end (Phase 9.2 theme; real dark mode)
- Built a real dark theme (was previously single-theme/light-only). Mechanism: src/settings/theme.ts exports applyTheme(theme) -> sets <html data-theme="dark|light">, plus normalizeTheme() + Theme type. index.css adds a [data-theme='dark'] block overriding the @theme color custom properties; since every Tailwind v4 color utility compiles to var(--color-*) and custom properties inherit from <html>, the override re-themes the entire app with no per-component changes.
- Persistence: appSettings.ts getTheme(db) (default 'light') / setTheme(db,theme), key 'theme'. SettingsScreen Theme Select now reads getTheme on mount and on change applies live (applyTheme) + writes setTheme. main.tsx restoreTheme() applies the persisted theme at boot (after restoreLockTimeout). Boot owns applying the persisted value; the screen applies live edits — that's why the SettingsScreen test asserts the DOM only after a change, not on mount.
- Theme change is NOT audited (cosmetic/non-security, consistent with lock timeout; only security-relevant settings like wllama CDN consent are audited).
- Tailwind v4 has NO config file — dark mode done purely via CSS var overrides under a data-theme selector ([data-theme='dark'] and :root have equal specificity, so source order wins → dark block placed after @theme/:root).
- Gate GREEN: typecheck clean; pnpm test 415 (+4: theme.test 2, appSettings 1, SettingsScreen 1); e2e 28.
- TODO: Phase 9.2 "theme" ticked end-to-end. The umbrella "read/write Settings from IndexedDB" boxes stay unticked (only theme + lock timeout wired so far). Remaining 9.2 settings (default/fallback provider, approval policy, backup, skill install policy, dev mode) still decorative/no-consumer.

## 2026-06-15T00:27:25Z - Claude Opus 4.8 - Fallback provider failover (Phase 9.2) — deliberate feature with real consumer
- Built provider failover end-to-end (user-approved design): on a primary LLM request failure, retry ONCE on a configured fallback provider, but only for reachability/transient errors.
- Error taxonomy (real kinds): cors|auth|model_not_found|unreachable|unknown (httpStatusToKind lumps 429/5xx into 'unknown'). New errors.ts isFailoverEligible(kind) = cors|unreachable|unknown; NOT auth/model_not_found (config errors a 2nd provider won't fix; silently trying another key on auth would mask the problem).
- Consumer = llmRunner: new resolveFallback?() dep returns FallbackTarget {id, provider, getApiKey} | null. attemptFallback() uses the fallback's OWN SecretVault key; FAIL-CLOSED — locked/missing fallback key => 'unavailable' (no silent unauthenticated call) and the ORIGINAL primary error surfaces. Success audits provider.failover_used (never silent). If the fallback also fails, the FALLBACK's error surfaces (last attempt). Restructured the catch into a FailoverOutcome union (ok/unavailable/failed); on 'ok' it sets text and falls through to the normal success path.
- main.tsx wires resolveFallback: getFallbackProviderId(db) -> skip if unset OR == active provider; resolve profile (persisted or default) + resolveProvider + resolveApiKey(secretVault, profile).
- appSettings: getFallbackProviderId/setFallbackProviderId (key 'fallbackProviderId', default null). SettingsScreen "Fallback provider" Select (None default + DEFAULT_PROVIDER_PROFILES); read on mount, write on change (not audited — config preference, like default provider).
- Gate GREEN: typecheck clean; pnpm test 431 (+9: errors 3, llmRunner 4, appSettings 1, SettingsScreen 1); e2e 28.
- GOTCHA: vitest passes type-loose tests; tsc caught a test helper returning Promise<unknown> for getApiKey — typed it as ApiKeyResolution / FallbackTarget. Always run pnpm run typecheck, not just vitest.
- 5 Settings controls now wired end-to-end: theme, default provider, fallback provider, approval policy, lock timeout. Remaining 9.2 (backup, skill install policy) lack consumers; dev mode env-gated.

## 2026-06-15T01:00:01Z - Claude Opus 4.8 - Passphrase-encrypted backup export/import (Phase 6.3) — deliberate crypto feature
- Built optional encrypted backup, REUSING the SecretVault crypto (no new crypto): backupService.encryptBackup(serialized, passphrase) -> deriveKey (PBKDF2-SHA256 250k) + encryptString (AES-256-GCM) -> self-contained file {format:'clawbackup-encrypted', v:1, salt, iv, ciphertext}. The ENTIRE serialized backup is ciphertext (no plaintext metadata leak). decryptBackup() reverses it; isEncryptedBackup() detects the format. Salt embedded so the file is portable; passphrase is INDEPENDENT of the vault passphrase (decryptable on another device).
- runBackupExport gained optional passphrase -> encrypts before download, filename browserclaw-backup.encrypted.clawbackup, audit summary "passphrase-encrypted". StorageScreen: new "Export Encrypted…" button -> passphrase+confirm dialog (min 8, must match). Import auto-detects encrypted files -> passphrase prompt -> decrypt -> existing validate/preview/restore path; wrong passphrase = AES-GCM auth failure -> "Incorrect passphrase". includeSecrets stays out of scope (separate item).
- Gate GREEN: typecheck clean; pnpm test 445 (+8); e2e 28. Ticked 6.3 "Add optional encrypted backup export" + "encrypted export can be imported".
- TESTING LESSON: RTL + PBKDF2 (250k iters) is FLAKY/slow in jsdom — a UI test doing multiple derivations timed out and showed a leaked double-render. Fix: cover the crypto SUCCESS path with a fast backupService UNIT test (encrypt->decrypt->parse->validate), and keep only ONE light UI test (wrong-passphrase prompt) for the interaction. Don't stack multiple PBKDF2 derivations in one RTL test.

- SCOPING CORRECTION: the "effect emitted/resolved/failed" + per-effect snapshot items were annotated "tied to effect-port contract (Phase 1.2, blocked)" — that was STALE. Every Effect already carries a stable id, and RuntimeHost.submit() sees both emitted effects (dispatch output) AND resolutions (resolve_effect commands flow back through submit). Gated by RuntimeHostOptions.auditEffects predicate.
- Setting plumbing: src/settings/runtimeAuditPref.ts holds a synchronous in-memory cache (the host's per-effect path can't await IndexedDB) with write-through: loadEffectAuditPref(db) at boot, setEffectAuditEnabled(db,v) updates cache+db. Key 'verboseRuntimeAudit'. main.tsx wires auditEffects: () => isEffectAuditEnabled() + boot load. SettingsScreen > Developer has a wired "Verbose runtime audit" toggle.
- Snapshot Phase 4 reconciliation: "after each effect emitted/resolved" marked COVERED, not literal-per-effect — port.snapshot() already exposes state; submit() debounce-saves after each turn AND each resolve_effect is its own submit (so per-resolution saves already happen, coalesced). Literal per-emitted-effect saves deliberately skipped (would thrash IndexedDB). "after runtime enters idle/error" stays unchecked — the runtime has no idle/error state machine (that WOULD need a Rust contract change).
- Gate GREEN: typecheck clean; pnpm test 451 (+4: runtimeHost 2, runtimeAuditPref 2); e2e 28. No cargo/WASM changes.

## 2026-06-15T03:59:24Z - Claude Opus 4.8 - Runtime idle/error snapshot triggers (Phase 4) — flush-on-error + reconcile, no Rust
- SCOPING outcome (user chose "TS flush-on-error + reconcile"): the "idle/error state machine" framing overstated the need. Idle = pending-effects map empty (derivable from port.snapshot().pending) and ALREADY persisted by the per-resolve debounced save. Error-execution is a HOST concern (the deterministic runtime never runs effects), so an idle/error status enum in claw-core would be speculative contract surface with no consumer — deliberately NOT added.
- Real gap fixed (TS only): RuntimeHost.submit() now wraps the effect loop in try/catch; on a fatal failEffect throw it calls #flushOnError() -> saveSnapshot (best-effort, gated on snapshots enabled) BEFORE re-throwing, so the turn's already-advanced runtime state isn't lost on reload. dispatch() advances state before the host executes effects, so flushing there persists the right state.

- Reconciled Phase 4 "Save snapshot:" — ALL sub-items now ticked (after user msg / each effect emitted+resolved / idle / error / before unload); ticked the parent. Corrected the stale "reference runtime has no idle/error state machine to hook" note.
- Gate GREEN: typecheck clean; pnpm test 452 (+1); e2e 28. No cargo/WASM.
